[\\"skipped\\" as const] : [])];\\n      if (!reasons.length) continue;\\n      result.push({\\n        key: `pt:${run.id}:${legIndex}:${question.qid}`, area: \\"lr\\", qid: question.qid,\\n        label: `PT${run.pt} · Section ${legIndex + 1} · Q${question.qnum}`, qtype: question.qtype || null,\\n        reasons, sources: [\\"practice_test\\"], firstAnswer: first, latestAnswer: answer(question.brAnswer) ?? first,\\n        correctAnswer: correct, firstMissAt: missed ? row.completed_at : null, lastActivityAt: row.completed_at,\\n        reflection: null, flaggedNote: null, href: href(run.id, legIndex, question.qid),\\n      });\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  const attemptsBySession = new Map<string, RcAttempt[]>();\\n  for (const attempt of rcAttempts) {\\n    const list = attemptsBySession.get(attempt.session_id) ?? []).sort((a, b) => a.created_at.localeCompare(b.created_at))) {\\n      if (seenQuestions.has(attempt.question_id)) continue;\\n      seenQuestions.add(attempt.question_id);\\n      const selected = answer(attempt.selected);\\n      const missed = !!selected && attempt.is_correct === false;\\n      const flagged = attempt.flagged === true;\\n      if (!missed && !flagged) continue;\\n      const qid = String(attempt.question_id);\\n      result.push({\\n        key: `pt:${run.id}:${legIndex}:${qid}`, area: \\"rc\\", qid,\\n        label: `PT${run.pt} · Section ${legIndex + 1} · RC question ${qid}`, qtype: null,\\n        reasons: [...(missed ? [\\"missed\\" as const] : []), ...(flagged ? [\\"flagged\\" as const] : [])],\\n        sources: [\\"practice_test\\"], firstAnswer: selected, latestAnswer: selected, correctAnswer: null,\\n        firstMissAt: missed ? attempt.created_at : null, lastActivityAt: session.completed_at,\\n        reflection: null, flaggedNote: null, href: href(run.id, legIndex, qid),\\n      });\\n    }\\n  }\\n  return result.sort((a, b) => b.lastActivityAt.localeCompare(a.lastActivityAt));\\n}\\n\\n/** Batch by sitting IDs, so opening Review never downloads every exam report or RC bank. */\\nexport async function loadPracticeTestReviewQueue(userId: string): Promise<ReviewQueueItem[]> {\\n  const runs = (await listSavedAccountPracticeTestRuns(userId)).filter((run) => run.userId === userId && run.status === \\"complete\\" && !hasOutdatedPt118Structure(run));\\n  if (!runs.length) return [];\\n  const runIds = runs.map((run) => run.id);\\n  const batches = await Promise.all(chunks(runIds).map(async (ids) => {\\n    const [lr, rc] = await Promise.all([\\n      supabase.from(\\"section_history\\").select(\\"practice_test_run_id, practice_test_leg, completed_at, questions_json\\")\\n        .eq(\\"user_id\\", userId).in(\\"practice_test_run_id\\", ids),\\n      supabase.from(\\"rc_drill_sessions\\").select(\\"id, practice_test_run_id, practice_test_leg, completed_at, passage_ids\\")\\n        .eq(\\"user_id\\", userId).eq(\\"status\\", \\"completed\\").in(\\"practice_test_run_id\\", ids),\\n    ]);\\n    if (lr.error) throw lr.error;\\n    if (rc.error) throw rc.error;\\n    return { lr: lr.data ?? Active flags resume practice without opening answer review. */\\nexport function buildRcReviewQueue(\\n  attempts: RcAttemptRow[],\\n  sessions: RcDrillSessionRow[],\\n  questionIndex: RcQuestionIndexEntry[],\\n): ReviewQueueItem[] {\\n  const validSessions = new Map(sessions\\n    .filter((session) => (session.status === \\"completed\\" || session.status === \\"in_progress\\") && !session.practice_test_run_id &&\\n      !(session.config && typeof session.config === \\"object\\" && \\"practiceTestRunId\\" in session.config && session.config.practiceTestRunId))\\n    .map((session) => [session.id, session]));\\n  const questions = new Map(questionIndex.map((question) => [question.questionId, question]));\\n  const groups = new Map<string, RcAttemptRow[]>();\\n\\n  for (const attempt of attempts) {\\n    const session = validSessions.get(attempt.session_id);\\n    const question = questions.get(attempt.question_id);\\n    if (!session || !question || attempt.user_id !== session.user_id || question.passageId !== attempt.passage_id || !session.passage_ids.includes(question.passageId)) continue;\\n    const key = `${attempt.session_id}:${attempt.question_id}`;\\n    const group = groups.get(key) ?? [];\\n    group.push(attempt);\\n    groups.set(key, group);\\n  }\\n\\n  const items: ReviewQueueItem[] = [];\\n  for (const [key, group] of groups) {\\n    group.sort((a, b) => a.created_at.localeCompare(b.created_at) || a.id.localeCompare(b.id));\\n    const first = group.find((attempt) => attempt.is_first_attempt) ?? group[0];\\n    const last = group.at(-1)!;\\n    const completed = validSessions.get(first.session_id)?.status === \\"completed\\";\\n    const firstMiss = group.find((attempt) => attempt.selected && attempt.is_correct === false);\\n    const flagged = last.flagged === true;\\n    if (!(completed && firstMiss) && !flagged) continue;\\n    const question = questions.get(first.question_id)!;\\n    const reasons: ReviewQueueItem[\\"reasons\\"] = [];\\n    if (completed && firstMiss) reasons.push(\\"missed\\");\\n    if (flagged) reasons.push(\\"flagged\\");\\n    items.push({\\n      key: `rc:${key}`,\\n      area: \\"rc\\",\\n      qid: String(question.questionId),\\n      label: `PT${question.preptest}–S${question.section}–Q${question.position}${completed ? \\"\\" : \\" · Active RC drill\\"}`,\\n      qtype: question.questionType || null,\\n      reasons,\\n      sources: [\\"rc_attempts\\"],\\n      firstAnswer: first.selected,\\n      latestAnswer: [...group].reverse().find((attempt) => attempt.selected)?.selected ?? null,\\n      correctAnswer: null,\\n      firstMissAt: completed ? firstMiss?.created_at ?? null : null,\\n      lastActivityAt: last.created_at,\\n      reflection: null,\\n      flaggedNote: null,\\n      href: `/rc/drill?sessionId=${encodeURIComponent(first.session_id)}${completed ? \\"&review=1\\" : \\"\\"}&q=${question.questionId}`,\\n      actionLabel: completed ? undefined : \\"Continue drill\\",\\n    });\\n  }\\n  return items.sort((a, b) => b.lastActivityAt.localeCompare(a.lastActivityAt));\\n}\\n"}'}]

## assistant

Review source audit on current main (same checked source as `2fa3319`):

| Source | Already works | Concrete remaining gap |
|---|---|---|
| **Bank LR wrong-answer journal** | `buildReviewQueue` merges WAJ history and independent `flagged_questions` by canonical qid; journal page loads question and autosaves wrong/correct reasoning and notes. Queue preserves exact filters/table/last-opened return. | WAJ is **question-wide**, not exact saved sitting/answer. Correct choice always visibly marked by `JournalQuestionView`/`JournalQuestionPane`, so no staged blind → reveal. No saved Review episode, completion removal, later-miss reopening or teacher selection. |
| **Smart LR** | Miss written into WAJ at answer time (`Drill.tsx` adaptive branch), feedback failure disclosed; flags saved independently. | Review only sees question-wide WAJ, not exact Smart answer ID/run. No exact-attempt episode or lifecycle. |
| **Custom/ordinary/guided LR drill runs** | `drillRunLedger.ts` saves UUID sitting in `bootcamp_kv.table_name=drill_runs`; validates outer owner, embedded session owner/id; guided closeout freezes attempts before analytics. | Review queue **does not load drill_runs directly**. Misses flow into WAJ, losing run identity; active flags are qid-wide. Need source adapter keyed `(runId,qid,answer identity)` before exact repeat/later miss lifecycle. |
| **Standalone official LR sections** | Completed standalone `section_history` items opened+left blank queue by `section:${historyId}:${qid}`; detail verifies owner and exact row/qid; initial/BR answer preserved. Answered misses reach WAJ. | Sitting adapter currently **only skipped LR**. Misses/flags not represented as exact section sitting tasks (WAJ may duplicate question-wide row). Detail reveals answer/explanation immediately. No episode completion/teacher selection. |
| **Standalone RC drill/section** | `rcReviewAdapter` groups by `(sessionId,questionId)`, validates attempt/session owner, canonical passage membership. Completed misses and flags queue; active flags continue without review reveal. Completed protected official RC read-only preserves answers, flags and analytics. | No saved Review episode / reasoning requirement / completion hide / teacher selection. Protected review cannot blind reanswer; ordinary RC blind reattempt exists, but writes another RC attempt rather than unified review event. No skipped RC task adapter (null selected does not qualify). |
| **Full PT LR** | `ptReviewSource` admits completed parent and leg only, exact run/leg/qid keys; missed/flagged/visited blank reasons; retained first/BR answers; opens targeted completed report. | No Review episode/lifecycle or teacher selection. Report displays outcomes/explanations immediately. |
| **Full PT RC** | Same completed-parent gate; saved session passage set must match frozen leg; first attempt per question; missed/flagged queue by run/leg/qid; report and protected RC detail preserve source answers. | No skipped RC handling, blind/reveal/diagnoses/completion episode or teacher selection. |

**Exact implemented episode boundary**

- `reviewEpisodes.ts`: `EpisodeSource = 'intro_lr_attempts' | 'hs_capstone'`.
- SQL artifact `2026-09-30-review-episodes.sql` CHECK has precisely those two.
- `boundedReviewBinding` only accepts native lab and Hard Sentences routes.
- `SavedReviewEpisodePanel` mounts only in `NativeLabReviewDetail` and `HardSentencesReviewDetail`.
- `loadScopedReviewSelections` loads only native lab + Hard Sentences. Teacher UI cannot select other source families.
- Existing service/event contract already handles retained immutable IDs, actor checks before/after transport, blind answer, reveal, reflection, complete; missed completion requires `error_diagnosis` and `right_answer_diagnosis`.
- Queue projector removes completed exact native/HS submissions while leaving original attempts/flags intact. Generalizing this is actual work, not “verify everything already exists.”

**Smallest shared execution contract recommendation**

1. Introduce a **typed source binding on `ReviewQueueItem`**, avoiding parsing detail URLs. Binding contains canonical owner, source family, immutable source attempt/sitting ID, question ID, source answer ID where needed; keep route independent.
2. Add read adapters for exact saved drill runs/section rows/RC session attempts/PT legs. Never manufacture an attempt ID from a legacy qid-only WAJ row. Keep such legacy rows available but label exact attempt unavailable.
3. Extend deployed episode source resolver server-side to validate each saved binding, completion eligibility, ownership and teacher admin role. Use exact tuple identity; derive question/first answer from source, never trust client supplied owner/question/result. Preserve source records and flags.
4. Reuse one episode panel on exact details: first answer display, optional blind answer, explicit reveal, two diagnoses for misses, account-acknowledged completion. Keep official source answer immutable. Use review events for blind picks, not new scored RC attempt rows.
5. Expand teacher selected-source reader to the same adapters and add reason; selected student scope must retain actor and owner separately.
6. Test later miss from **new attempt** reappears; completing one sitting does not remove another; flags survive review completion; user/account/route changes cannot confirm other student work; return state holds.
7. Run one ordinary student + teacher real task walk per transport family (bank/drill KV, section history, RC session, PT) after deploy. Existing queue filter/responsive checks need not be rebuilt.

**Acceptance evidence already exists:** published 2026-10-02 Review integrity release, 87 focused tests, actual owned LR journal route return/reload and filter/table marker in fresh production. Full PT/RC official reports and source protection browser evidence exists separately. None proves broad Review completion because the episode contract itself does not support these families yet.

One caution: legacy journal history’s `firstAnswer = history[0]` need not equal first-ever scored answer across all transport families; exact source adapters should use original immutable saved answer, not label global WAJ first as an exact sitting answer.

Today\'s\\n *    coupled reveal, untouched.\\n *\\n * A pure function of the resolved spec, so an early render can never\\n * misclassify a run the way the mutable `isPracticeSetMode` state could.\\n */\\nexport type ReviewPolicy = \'fixed\' | \'adaptive\' | \'none\';\\n\\n/**\\n * Which pick model a run uses. `list`: a pick is written straight into\\n * `session.attempts` and the set is persisted at the end. `commit`: a pick\\n * sits in `selectedAnswer` and is persisted per question on the way out.\\n * Independent of the review policy, because a `questions` launch can arrive as\\n * `natural-drill` and never sets a selected-qid list.\\n */\\nexport type PickModel = \'list\' | \'commit\';\\n\\nfunction policyForKind(kind: DrillLaunchBody[\'kind\']): ReviewPolicy {\\n  switch (kind) {\\n    case \'guided-drill\':\\n    case \'assignment\':\\n    case \'saved-set\':\\n    case \'questions\':\\n    case \'practice-set\':\\n    case \'bootcamp-day\':\\n    case \'filter\':\\n      return \'fixed\';\\n    case \'smart-drill\':\\n      return \'adaptive\';\\n    case \'section\':\\n      return \'none\';\\n    default: {\\n      const never: never = kind;\\n      return never;\\n    }\\n  }\\n}\\n\\nexport function reviewPolicyForRun(spec: DrillRunSpec): ReviewPolicy {\\n  if (spec.sealed || spec.ptRun) return \'none\';\\n  if (!spec.launchKind) return \'none\';\\n  return policyForKind(spec.launchKind);\\n}\\n\\n/** The exact condition the runner uses to enter the in-memory-attempt model. */\\nexport function hasSelectedQidList(spec: DrillRunSpec): boolean {\\n  if (spec.mode !== \'type-drill\') return false;\\n  const config = spec.config as { selectedQids?: string[] } | undefined;\\n  return !!config?.selectedQids && config.selectedQids.length > 0;\\n}\\n\\nexport function pickModelForRun(spec: DrillRunSpec): PickModel {\\n  return hasSelectedQidList(spec) ? No high-confidence blocker found in the reviewed LR changes.

Verified:

- Explicit Continue loads the account-owned saved row before resolving the runner.
- Frozen source key and exact queue survive resume; saved runs skip random draw and dedupe.
- List/commit restoration matches `pickModelForRun`: commit resumes omit `selectedQids`; list resumes retain them.
- Saved official/classic layout overrides URL layout.
- Ledger checks source/mode consistency and freezes existing attempt provenance. Historical answers without metadata stay unknown.
- Provenance labels disclose missing historical settings in both runner and Results/Review.
- Completed saved runs redirect to Results.
- Missing bank question fails with preserved saved-answer disclosure.
- Launch resolution guards cancelled account/route loads; ledger authenticated owner checks protect mutations.
